```ts
import { EventEmitter, once } from "node:events";
import { mkdtempSync, rmSync } from "node:fs";
import { tmpdir } from "node:os";
import { join } from "node:path";
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import type { FastifyInstance } from "fastify";
import WebSocket from "ws";
import { specSchema } from "@mcsm/shared";
import { buildApp } from "../app.js";
import { openDb } from "../db/db.js";
import { JobsRepo, type JobRow } from "./repo.js";
import { JobService, MAX_JOB_LOG } from "./service.js";

function makeService() {
  const db = openDb(":memory:");
  const events = new EventEmitter();
  const seen: unknown[][] = [];
  events.on("job", (...args: unknown[]) => seen.push(args));
  const repo = new JobsRepo(db);
  return { svc: new JobService(repo, events), repo, seen };
}

describe("JobService", () => {
  it("crea, avanza y termina emitiendo eventos; un job terminado ya no cambia", () => {
    const { svc, seen } = makeService();
    const j = svc.create({ type: "backup", status: "running", message: "Solicitando" });
    expect(j).toMatchObject({ status: "running", progress: 0, message: "Solicitando" });
    expect(j.startedAt).not.toBeNull();
    svc.update(j.id, { progress: 40, message: "Copiando", logChunk: "save-off\n" });
    svc.update(j.id, { logChunk: "save-all flush\n" });
    svc.finish(j.id, "ok", "Copia creada");
    svc.update(j.id, { status: "failed", message: "tarde" });
    const d = svc.get(j.id)!;
    expect(d).toMatchObject({ status: "ok", progress: 100, message: "Copia creada", log: "save-off\nsave-all flush\n" });
    expect(d.finishedAt).not.toBeNull();
    expect(seen).toHaveLength(4); // create + 2 avances + finish; el aviso tardío no emite
    expect(seen[1]![1]).toBe("save-off\n");
  });

  it("un log completo sustituye al guardado", () => {
    const { svc } = makeService();
    const j = svc.create({ type: "backup", status: "running" });
    svc.update(j.id, { logChunk: "parcial\n" });
    svc.update(j.id, { log: "completo\n", status: "ok" });
    expect(svc.get(j.id)!.log).toBe("completo\n");
  });

  it("recorta el log a 256 KiB conservando el final", () => {
    const { svc } = makeService();
    const j = svc.create({ type: "backup", status: "running" });
    const line = "x".repeat(1023) + "\n";
    for (let i = 0; i < 300; i++) svc.update(j.id, { logChunk: line });
    svc.update(j.id, { logChunk: "ULTIMA\n" });
    const log = svc.get(j.id)!.log;
    expect(log.length).toBeLessThanOrEqual(MAX_JOB_LOG);
    expect(log.startsWith("…(recortado)")).toBe(true);
    expect(log.endsWith("ULTIMA\n")).toBe(true);
  });

  it("lista los más nuevos primero y filtra por estado", () => {
    const { svc, repo } = makeService();
    const base: Omit<JobRow, "id" | "created_at" | "status"> = {
      type: "backup", server_id: null, node_id: null, progress: 0, message: "", log: "", payload: "{}", updated_at: "", started_at: null, finished_at: null,
    };
    repo.insert({ ...base, id: "a", status: "ok", created_at: "2026-10-12T04:00:00.000Z" });
    repo.insert({ ...base, id: "b", status: "running", created_at: "2026-10-12T05:00:00.000Z" });
    repo.insert({ ...base, id: "c", status: "ok", created_at: "2026-10-12T06:00:00.000Z" });
    expect(svc.list({}).map((j) => j.id)).toEqual(["c", "b", "a"]);
    expect(svc.list({ status: "ok" }).map((j) => j.id)).toEqual(["c", "a"]);
    expect(svc.list({ before: "2026-10-12T06:00:00.000Z", limit: 1 }).map((j) => j.id)).toEqual(["b"]);
  });

  it("purga solo los terminados antiguos", () => {
    const { svc } = makeService();
    const done = svc.create({ type: "backup", status: "running" });
    svc.finish(done.id, "ok", "hecho");
    const open = svc.create({ type: "backup", status: "running" });
    expect(svc.purgeFinishedBefore(new Date(Date.now() + 1000).toISOString())).toBe(1);
    expect(svc.get(done.id)).toBeNull();
    expect(svc.get(open.id)).not.toBeNull();
  });
});

describe("reinicio del panel", () => {
  let dir: string;
  beforeEach(() => { dir = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), "mcsm-jobs-")); });
  afterEach(() => rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }));

  it("los jobs a medias pasan a failed «interrumpido» salvo backup y restore", async () => {
    const path = join(dir, "panel.db");
    const app1 = await buildApp({ db: openDb(path) });
    await app1.ready();
    const applying = app1.jobs.create({ type: "apply_spec", status: "running" });
    const queued = app1.jobs.create({ type: "migrate" });
    const backup = app1.jobs.create({ type: "backup", status: "running" });
    const restore = app1.jobs.create({ type: "restore", status: "running" });
    const done = app1.jobs.create({ type: "apply_spec", status: "running" });
    app1.jobs.finish(done.id, "ok", "hecho");
    await app1.close();

    const app2 = await buildApp({ db: openDb(path) });
    await app2.ready();
    expect(app2.jobs.get(applying.id)).toMatchObject({ status: "failed", message: expect.stringMatching(/interrumpido/) });
    expect(app2.jobs.get(queued.id)!.status).toBe("failed");
    expect(app2.jobs.get(backup.id)!.status).toBe("running");
    expect(app2.jobs.get(restore.id)!.status).toBe("running");
    expect(app2.jobs.get(done.id)).toMatchObject({ status: "ok", message: "hecho" });
    await app2.close();
  });
});

  const get = (u: string) => app.inject({ method: "GET", url: u, headers: { cookie } });

  it("exige sesión", async () => {
    expect((await app.inject({ method: "GET", url: "/api/jobs" })).statusCode).toBe(401);
  });

  it("lista con filtros y devuelve el detalle con log", async () => {
    const t = (await app.inject({ method: "POST", url: "/api/nodes/enrollment-tokens", headers: { cookie }, payload: { nameHint: "nas" } })).json().token;
    const { nodeId } = (await app.inject({ method: "POST", url: "/agent/enroll", payload: { enrollToken: t, nameHint: "nas", agentVersion: "fake" } })).json();
    const spec = specSchema.parse({ type: "PAPER", version: "1.21.4", memory: "2G", ports: { game: 25565 }, rconPassword: "abcdefgh" });
    const s = app.servers.create({ nodeId, name: "Uno", slug: "uno", spec, desiredState: "running" });
    const mine = app.jobs.create({ type: "backup", serverId: s.id, nodeId, status: "running" });
    app.jobs.update(mine.id, { logChunk: "save-off\n" });
    const other = app.jobs.create({ type: "backup", status: "running" });
    app.jobs.finish(other.id, "failed", "falló");

    let res = await get(`/api/jobs?serverId=${s.id}`);
    expect(res.statusCode).toBe(200);
    expect(res.json().map((j: any) => j.id)).toEqual([mine.id]);
    expect(res.json()[0].log).toBeUndefined();
    res = await get("/api/jobs?status=failed");
    expect(res.json().map((j: any) => j.id)).toEqual([other.id]);
    expect((await get("/api/jobs?status=raro")).statusCode).toBe(400);
    res = await get(`/api/jobs/${mine.id}`);
    expect(res.json()).toMatchObject({ id: mine.id, type: "backup", serverId: s.id, status: "running", log: "save-off\n" });
    expect((await get("/api/jobs/no-existe")).statusCode).toBe(404);
  });

  it("publica los cambios de jobs y de copias en el topic jobs del WebSocket", async () => {
    const ws = new WebSocket(url.replace("http", "ws") + "/ws", { headers: { cookie } });
    const queue: any[] = [];
    let wake: (() => void) | null = null;
    ws.on("message", (d) => { queue.push(JSON.parse(d.toString())); wake?.(); });
    const next = async () => {
      while (queue.length === 0) await new Promise<void>((r) => { wake = r; });
      return queue.shift();
    };
    await once(ws, "open");
    ws.send(JSON.stringify({ type: "subscribe", topic: "jobs" }));
    // El hub atiende los mensajes en orden: el error de este llega después de procesar la suscripción.
    ws.send(JSON.stringify({ type: "subscribe", topic: "nope" }));
    expect(await next()).toMatchObject({ type: "error" });
    const j = app.jobs.create({ type: "backup", status: "running" });
    expect(await next()).toMatchObject({ type: "job", job: { id: j.id, status: "running" }, logChunk: "" });
    app.jobs.update(j.id, { logChunk: "save-off\n" });
    expect(await next()).toMatchObject({ type: "job", logChunk: "save-off\n" });
    app.events.emit("backups", "srv-1");
    expect(await next()).toEqual({ type: "backups", serverId: "srv-1" });
    ws.close();
  });
});
```

`panel/apps/api/src/jobs/repo.ts`:
```ts
import type { Db } from "../db/db.js";

export type JobState = "queued" | "running" | "ok" | "failed" | "cancelled";

export interface JobRow {
  id: string; type: string; server_id: string | null; node_id: string | null; status: JobState;
  progress: number; message: string; log: string; payload: string;
  created_at: string; updated_at: string; started_at: string | null; finished_at: string | null;
}

export interface JobDto {
  id: string; type: string; serverId: string | null; nodeId: string | null; status: JobState;
  progress: number; message: string; payload: Record<string, unknown>;
  createdAt: string; updatedAt: string; startedAt: string | null; finishedAt: string | null;
}

/** Detalle con el log (los listados no lo llevan: puede ocupar 256 KiB). */
export interface JobDetailDto extends JobDto { log: string }

export interface JobListFilter { serverId?: string; status?: JobState; limit?: number; before?: string }

export function toJobDto(r: JobRow): JobDto {
  return {
    id: r.id, type: r.type, serverId: r.server_id, nodeId: r.node_id, status: r.status, progress: r.progress,
    message: r.message, payload: JSON.parse(r.payload) as Record<string, unknown>,
    createdAt: r.created_at, updatedAt: r.updated_at, startedAt: r.started_at, finishedAt: r.finished_at,
  };
}

export function toJobDetailDto(r: JobRow): JobDetailDto {
  return { ...toJobDto(r), log: r.log };
}

export class JobsRepo {
  constructor(private db: Db) {}

  insert(r: JobRow): void {
    this.db.prepare(`INSERT INTO jobs (id, type, server_id, node_id, status, progress, message, log, payload, created_at, updated_at, started_at, finished_at)
      VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`)
      .run(r.id, r.type, r.server_id, r.node_id, r.status, r.progress, r.message, r.log, r.payload, r.created_at, r.updated_at, r.started_at, r.finished_at);
  }

  /** Guarda los campos que cambian con el avance. */
  save(r: JobRow): void {
    this.db.prepare(`UPDATE jobs SET status = ?, progress = ?, message = ?, log = ?, started_at = ?, finished_at = ?, updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?`)
      .run(r.status, r.progress, r.message, r.log, r.started_at, r.finished_at, r.updated_at, r.id);
  }

  get(id: string): JobRow | null {
    return (this.db.prepare("SELECT * FROM jobs WHERE id = ?").get(id) as JobRow | undefined) ?? null;
  }

  /** Los más nuevos primero; como mucho 200. */
  list(f: JobListFilter): JobRow[] {
    const where: string[] = [];
    const args: (string | number)[] = [];
    if (f.serverId) { where.push("server_id = ?"); args.push(f.serverId); }
    if (f.status) { where.push("status = ?"); args.push(f.status); }
    if (f.before) { where.push("created_at < ?"); args.push(f.before); }
    args.push(Math.min(Math.max(f.limit ?? 50, 1), 200));
    const sql = `SELECT * FROM jobs ${where.length ? `WHERE ${where.join(" AND ")}` : ""} ORDER BY created_at DESC, id DESC LIMIT ?`;
    return this.db.prepare(sql).all(...args) as unknown as JobRow[];
  }

  /** Marca failed los jobs abiertos cuyo tipo no esté en `exceptTypes`. Devuelve sus ids. */
  failOpen(exceptTypes: readonly string[], message: string, now: string): string[] {
    const ph = exceptTypes.map(() => "?").join(", ");
    const rows = this.db.prepare(`SELECT id FROM jobs WHERE status IN ('queued', 'running') AND type NOT IN (${ph})`).all(...exceptTypes) as unknown as { id: string }[];
    const stmt = this.db.prepare("UPDATE jobs SET status = 'failed', message = ?, finished_at = ?, updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?");
    for (const r of rows) stmt.run(message, now, now, r.id);
    return rows.map((r) => r.id);
  }

  /** Jobs abiertos de un nodo, de los tipos dados, creados antes de `createdBefore`. */
  openForNode(nodeId: string, types: readonly string[], createdBefore: string): JobRow[] {
    const ph = types.map(() => "?").join(", ");
    return this.db.prepare(`SELECT * FROM jobs WHERE node_id = ? AND status IN ('queued', 'running') AND type IN (${ph}) AND created_at < ?`)
      .all(nodeId, ...types, createdBefore) as unknown as JobRow[];
  }

  purgeFinishedBefore(iso: string): number {
    return Number(this.db.prepare("DELETE FROM jobs WHERE finished_at IS NOT NULL AND finished_at < ?").run(iso).changes);
  }
}
```

`panel/apps/api/src/jobs/service.ts`:
```ts
import type { EventEmitter } from "node:events";
import { newId } from "../lib/ids.js";
import { nowIso } from "../lib/time.js";
import { JobsRepo, toJobDetailDto, toJobDto, type JobDetailDto, type JobDto, type JobListFilter, type JobRow, type JobState } from "./repo.js";

export const FINAL_STATES: ReadonlySet<JobState> = new Set<JobState>(["ok", "failed", "cancelled"]);
/** Jobs que ejecuta el agente: sobreviven a un reinicio del panel; el agente informa al reconectar (spec §10). */
export const AGENT_JOB_TYPES = ["backup", "restore"] as const;
export const MAX_JOB_LOG = 256 * 1024;
export const INTERRUPTED = "interrumpido: el panel se reinició durante el job";

export interface CreateJobInput {
  id?: string; type: string; serverId?: string | null; nodeId?: string | null;
  status?: "queued" | "running"; message?: string; payload?: Record<string, unknown>;
}

export interface JobUpdate {
  status?: JobState; progress?: number; message?: string;
  /** Líneas nuevas que se añaden al log. */
  logChunk?: string;
  /** Log completo que sustituye al guardado (inventario del agente). */
  log?: string;
}

function capLog(log: string): string {
  if (log.length <= MAX_JOB_LOG) return log;
  const mark = "…(recortado)\n";
  return mark + log.slice(log.length - MAX_JOB_LOG + mark.length);
}

/** Jobs genéricos con progreso y log (spec §7). Cada cambio emite `events.emit("job", dto, logChunk)`. */
export class JobService {
  constructor(private repo: JobsRepo, private events: EventEmitter) {}

  create(input: CreateJobInput): JobDto {
    const now = nowIso();
    const status = input.status ?? "queued";
    const row: JobRow = {
      id: input.id ?? newId(), type: input.type, server_id: input.serverId ?? null, node_id: input.nodeId ?? null,
      status, progress: 0, message: input.message ?? "", log: "", payload: JSON.stringify(input.payload ?? {}),
      created_at: now, updated_at: now, started_at: status === "running" ? now : null, finished_at: null,
    };
    this.repo.insert(row);
    return this.emit(row, "");
  }

  /** Aplica un avance. Un job terminado ya no cambia: un aviso tardío no lo reabre. */
  update(id: string, u: JobUpdate): JobDto | null {
    const row = this.repo.get(id);
    if (!row) return null;
    if (FINAL_STATES.has(row.status)) return toJobDto(row);
    const now = nowIso();
    const next: JobRow = { ...row, updated_at: now };
    if (u.status) {
      next.status = u.status;
      if (u.status === "running" && !next.started_at) next.started_at = now;
      if (FINAL_STATES.has(u.status)) next.finished_at = now;
    }
    if (u.progress !== undefined) next.progress = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, Math.round(u.progress)));
    if (u.message !== undefined) next.message = u.message;
    if (u.log !== undefined) next.log = capLog(u.log);
    else if (u.logChunk) next.log = capLog(next.log + u.logChunk);
    if (next.status === "ok") next.progress = 100;
    this.repo.save(next);
    return this.emit(next, u.log !== undefined ? "" : (u.logChunk ?? ""));
  }

  finish(id: string, status: "ok" | "failed" | "cancelled", message: string): JobDto | null {
    return this.update(id, { status, message });
  }

  get(id: string): JobDetailDto | null {
    const r = this.repo.get(id);
    return r ? toJobDetailDto(r) : null;
  }

  getRow(id: string): JobRow | null {
    return this.repo.get(id);
  }

  list(f: JobListFilter): JobDto[] {
    return this.repo.list(f).map(toJobDto);
  }

  /** Al arrancar el panel: los jobs a medias pasan a failed "interrumpido", salvo los del agente (spec §10). */
  interruptStale(): number {
    const ids = this.repo.failOpen(AGENT_JOB_TYPES, INTERRUPTED, nowIso());
    for (const id of ids) {
      const r = this.repo.get(id);
      if (r) this.emit(r, "");
    }
    return ids.length;
  }

  /** Jobs del agente (backup, restore) de un nodo, aún abiertos y creados antes de `createdBefore`. */
  openAgentJobs(nodeId: string, createdBefore: string): JobRow[] {
    return this.repo.openForNode(nodeId, AGENT_JOB_TYPES, createdBefore);
  }

  purgeFinishedBefore(iso: string): number {
    return this.repo.purgeFinishedBefore(iso);
  }

  private emit(row: JobRow, logChunk: string): JobDto {
    const dto = toJobDto(row);
    this.events.emit("job", dto, logChunk);
    return dto;
  }
}
```

`panel/apps/api/src/jobs/routes.ts`:
```ts
import fp from "fastify-plugin";
import type { FastifyError, FastifyInstance } from "fastify";
import { z } from "zod";
import { JobsRepo } from "./repo.js";
import { JobService } from "./service.js";

const PURGE_AFTER_DAYS = 30;
const PURGE_EVERY_MS = 60 * 60 * 1000;

const listQuery = z.object({
  serverId: z.string().min(1).optional(),
  status: z.enum(["queued", "running", "ok", "failed", "cancelled"]).optional(),
  limit: z.coerce.number().int().min(1).max(200).optional(),
  before: z.string().min(1).optional(),
});

/** Decora `app.jobs` en la raíz y registra las rutas encapsuladas de /api/jobs. */
export const jobRoutes = fp(async function jobRoutes(root: FastifyInstance) {
  const svc = new JobService(new JobsRepo(root.db), root.events);
  root.decorate("jobs", svc);
  const purge = () => svc.purgeFinishedBefore(new Date(Date.now() - PURGE_AFTER_DAYS * 86_400_000).toISOString());
  let timer: NodeJS.Timeout | undefined;
  root.addHook("onReady", async () => {
    const n = svc.interruptStale();
    if (n > 0) root.log.warn({ count: n }, "jobs interrumpidos por el reinicio del panel");
    purge();
    timer = setInterval(purge, PURGE_EVERY_MS);
    timer.unref();
  });
  root.addHook("onClose", async () => { if (timer) clearInterval(timer); });
  await root.register(jobRoutesInner);
});

async function jobRoutesInner(app: FastifyInstance) {
  app.setErrorHandler((err: FastifyError | Error, _req, reply) => {
    if (err instanceof z.ZodError) return reply.code(400).send({ error: err.issues[0]?.message ?? "Datos inválidos" });
    const status = (err as FastifyError).statusCode;
    if (status === undefined || status >= 500) {
      app.log.error(err);
      return reply.code(500).send({ error: "Error interno" });
    }
    return reply.code(status).send({ error: err.message });
  });

  app.get("/api/jobs", async (req) => app.jobs.list(listQuery.parse(req.query)));

  app.get<{ Params: { id: string } }>("/api/jobs/:id", async (req, reply) => {
    const job = app.jobs.get(req.params.id);
    if (!job) return reply.code(404).send({ error: "Job no encontrado" });
    return job;
  });
}

declare module "fastify" {
  interface FastifyInstance { jobs: JobService }
}
```

- Sincronización: tras cada `Hello` se envía `BackupList{serverId: ""}`; el `BackupInventory` (id 0) inserta/actualiza las copias de los servidores de `server_ids` y marca `deleted` las que faltan, crea o completa los jobs del agente (log completo) y da por perdidos (`failed` "interrumpido…") los jobs `backup`/`restore` abiertos de ese nodo creados **antes** de pedir el inventario que el agente no conoce. `JobProgress` de un job desconocido de un servidor del nodo crea el job (`payload: { trigger: "scheduled" }`). Se ignora todo aviso sobre servidores o jobs de otro nodo. Cada cambio de copias emite `app.events.emit("backups", serverId)`.

```ts
import type { EventEmitter } from "node:events";
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import type { FastifyInstance } from "fastify";
import type { MessageInitShape } from "@bufbuild/protobuf";
import { BackupTrigger, JobStatus, type BackupInfoSchema, type Envelope } from "@mcsm/proto";
import { specSchema } from "@mcsm/shared";
import { buildApp } from "../app.js";
import { openDb } from "../db/db.js";
import { FakeAgent } from "../../test/fake-agent.js";

let app: FastifyInstance; let url: string; let cookie: string;

const baseSpec = specSchema.parse({ type: "PAPER", version: "1.21.4", memory: "2G", ports: { game: 25565 }, rconPassword: "abcdefgh" });

beforeEach(async () => {
  app = await buildApp({ db: openDb(":memory:") });
  await app.listen({ port: 0, host: "127.0.0.1" });
  url = `http://127.0.0.1:${(app.server.address() as any).port}`;
  const res = await app.inject({ method: "POST", url: "/api/auth/setup", payload: { password: "correct horse" } });
  const c = res.cookies.find((c) => c.name === "mcsm_session")!;
  cookie = `${c.name}=${c.value}`;
});
afterEach(async () => { await app.close(); });

async function enrollNode(name: string): Promise<{ nodeId: string; nodeToken: string }> {
  const t = (await app.inject({ method: "POST", url: "/api/nodes/enrollment-tokens", headers: { cookie }, payload: { nameHint: name } })).json().token;
  return (await app.inject({ method: "POST", url: "/agent/enroll", payload: { enrollToken: t, nameHint: name, agentVersion: "fake" } })).json();
}

const getBackups = async (serverId: string) => (await app.inject({ method: "GET", url: `/api/servers/${serverId}/backups`, headers: { cookie } })).json();

/** Espera el siguiente evento `name` que cumpla `pred`. Regístralo ANTES de provocarlo. */
function nextEvent(em: EventEmitter, name: string, pred: (...args: any[]) => boolean = () => true): Promise<unknown[]> {
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const timer = setTimeout(() => { em.off(name, h); reject(new Error(`sin evento ${name}`)); }, 3000);
    const h = (...args: any[]) => {
      if (!pred(...args)) return;
      clearTimeout(timer);
      em.off(name, h);
      resolve(args);
    };
    em.on(name, h);
  });
}

/** Conecta un agente falso, confirma el SyncSpecs y devuelve la petición de inventario (llegan en cualquier orden). */
async function connectAgent(nodeToken: string, specVersions: Record<string, bigint> = {}) {
  const agent = new FakeAgent(url, nodeToken);
  await agent.connect({ specVersions });
  const seen = new Map<string, Envelope>();
  while (!seen.has("syncSpecs") || !seen.has("backupList")) {
    const e = await agent.next();
    if (e.body.case) seen.set(e.body.case, e);
  }
  agent.ack(seen.get("syncSpecs")!.id);
  return { agent, list: seen.get("backupList")! };
}

type InfoInit = MessageInitShape<typeof BackupInfoSchema>;
const T0 = 1_760_000_000_000n; // ms
const info = (serverId: string, filename: string, over: Partial<InfoInit> = {}): InfoInit => ({
  serverId, filename, sizeBytes: 1234n, sha256: "ab".repeat(32), specVersion: 1n, trigger: BackupTrigger.SCHEDULED,
  startedUnixMs: T0, finishedUnixMs: T0 + 5000n, ...over,
});

describe("inventario y avisos del agente", () => {
  it("al conectar pide el inventario y lo refleja en copias y jobs", async () => {
    const { nodeId, nodeToken } = await enrollNode("nas");
    const s = app.servers.create({ nodeId, name: "Uno", slug: "uno", spec: baseSpec, desiredState: "running" });
    const { agent, list } = await connectAgent(nodeToken, { [s.id]: 1n });
    if (list.body.case !== "backupList") throw new Error();
    expect(list.body.value.serverId).toBe("");
    const changed = nextEvent(app.events, "backups", (id) => id === s.id);
    agent.ack(list.id);
    agent.send({ body: { case: "backupInventory", value: {
      serverIds: [s.id],
      backups: [
        info(s.id, "uno-20261012T040000.000Z-1.tar.zst"),
        info(s.id, "uno-20261012T050000.000Z-1.tar.zst", { startedUnixMs: T0 + 3_600_000n }),
        info(s.id, "data.pre-restore-20261012T060000.000Z", { trigger: BackupTrigger.PRE_RESTORE, sha256: "", startedUnixMs: T0 + 7_200_000n }),
      ],
      jobs: [{ jobId: "job-agente", serverId: s.id, type: "backup", status: JobStatus.OK, progress: 100, message: "Copia creada", log: "save-off\nsave-on\n", startedUnixMs: 1n, finishedUnixMs: 2n }],
    } } });
    await changed;
    const rows = await getBackups(s.id);
    expect(rows.map((b: any) => b.filename)).toEqual([
      "data.pre-restore-20261012T060000.000Z", "uno-20261012T050000.000Z-1.tar.zst", "uno-20261012T040000.000Z-1.tar.zst",
    ]);
    expect(rows[0]).toMatchObject({ trigger: "pre_restore", kind: "directory" });
    expect(rows[1]).toMatchObject({ trigger: "scheduled", kind: "archive", sizeBytes: 1234, status: "ok", specVersion: 1 });
    expect(app.jobs.get("job-agente")).toMatchObject({ type: "backup", serverId: s.id, status: "ok", progress: 100, log: "save-off\nsave-on\n" });
    agent.close();
  });

  it("marca borradas las copias que ya no están y da por perdidos los jobs abiertos que el agente no conoce", async () => {
    const { nodeId, nodeToken } = await enrollNode("nas");
    const s = app.servers.create({ nodeId, name: "Uno", slug: "uno", spec: baseSpec, desiredState: "running" });
    let { agent, list } = await connectAgent(nodeToken, { [s.id]: 1n });
    let changed = nextEvent(app.events, "backups", (id) => id === s.id);
    agent.ack(list.id);
    agent.send({ body: { case: "backupInventory", value: { serverIds: [s.id], backups: [info(s.id, "uno-20261012T040000.000Z-1.tar.zst")], jobs: [] } } });
    await changed;
    expect(await getBackups(s.id)).toHaveLength(1);
    const lost = app.jobs.create({ type: "backup", serverId: s.id, nodeId, status: "running" });
    agent.close();

    ({ agent, list } = await connectAgent(nodeToken, { [s.id]: 1n }));
    const late = app.jobs.create({ type: "backup", serverId: s.id, nodeId, status: "running" }); // posterior a la petición
    changed = nextEvent(app.events, "backups", (id) => id === s.id);
    agent.ack(list.id);
    agent.send({ body: { case: "backupInventory", value: { serverIds: [s.id], backups: [], jobs: [] } } });
    await changed;
    expect(await getBackups(s.id)).toEqual([]);
    expect(app.jobs.get(lost.id)).toMatchObject({ status: "failed", message: expect.stringMatching(/interrumpido/) });
    expect(app.jobs.get(late.id)!.status).toBe("running");
    agent.close();
  });

  it("un JobProgress desconocido crea el job programado; BackupCreated y BackupDeleted actualizan la lista", async () => {
    const { nodeId, nodeToken } = await enrollNode("nas");
    const s = app.servers.create({ nodeId, name: "Uno", slug: "uno", spec: baseSpec, desiredState: "running" });
    const { agent, list } = await connectAgent(nodeToken, { [s.id]: 1n });
    agent.ack(list.id);
    const done = nextEvent(app.events, "job", (j) => j.id === "job-prog" && j.status === "ok");
    const progress = (status: JobStatus, extra: object) => ({ body: { case: "jobProgress" as const, value: { jobId: "job-prog", serverId: s.id, type: "backup", status, ...extra } } });
    agent.send(progress(JobStatus.RUNNING, { progress: 1, message: "Desactivando", logChunk: "save-off\n" }));
    agent.send({ body: { case: "backupCreated", value: { backup: info(s.id, "uno-20261012T040000.000Z-1.tar.zst"), jobId: "job-prog" } } });
    agent.send(progress(JobStatus.OK, { progress: 100, message: "Copia creada", logChunk: "save-on\n" }));
    await done;
    expect(app.jobs.get("job-prog")).toMatchObject({ type: "backup", serverId: s.id, nodeId, status: "ok", payload: { trigger: "scheduled" }, log: "save-off\nsave-on\n" });
    expect(await getBackups(s.id)).toHaveLength(1);
    const gone = nextEvent(app.events, "backups", (id) => id === s.id);
    agent.send({ body: { case: "backupDeleted", value: { serverId: s.id, filename: "uno-20261012T040000.000Z-1.tar.zst", reason: "poda" } } });
    await gone;
    expect(await getBackups(s.id)).toEqual([]);
    agent.close();
  });

  it("ignora avisos sobre servidores de otro nodo", async () => {
    const a = await enrollNode("a");
    const b = await enrollNode("b");
    const sa = app.servers.create({ nodeId: a.nodeId, name: "A", slug: "a", spec: baseSpec, desiredState: "running" });
    const sb = app.servers.create({ nodeId: b.nodeId, name: "B", slug: "b", spec: baseSpec, desiredState: "running" });
    const { agent, list } = await connectAgent(a.nodeToken, { [sa.id]: 1n });
    agent.ack(list.id);
    const barrier = nextEvent(app.events, "backups", (id) => id === sa.id);
    agent.send({ body: { case: "backupCreated", value: { backup: info(sb.id, "b-20261012T040000.000Z-1.tar.zst") } } });
    agent.send({ body: { case: "jobProgress", value: { jobId: "ajeno", serverId: sb.id, type: "backup", status: JobStatus.RUNNING } } });
    agent.send({ body: { case: "backupCreated", value: { backup: info(sa.id, "a-20261012T040000.000Z-1.tar.zst") } } });
    await barrier;
    expect(await getBackups(sb.id)).toEqual([]);
    expect(app.jobs.get("ajeno")).toBeNull();
    agent.close();
  });
});
```

`panel/apps/api/src/backups/repo.ts`:
```ts
import type { Db } from "../db/db.js";
import { newId } from "../lib/ids.js";
import { nowIso } from "../lib/time.js";

export type BackupTriggerName = "manual" | "scheduled" | "pre_migration" | "pre_restore";
export type BackupStatus = "running" | "ok" | "failed" | "deleted";

export interface BackupRow {
  id: string; server_id: string; node_id: string; filename: string; size_bytes: number; sha256: string;
  spec_version: number; trigger: BackupTriggerName; status: BackupStatus; started_at: string;
  finished_at: string | null; error: string | null; created_at: string;
}

export interface BackupDto {
  id: string; serverId: string; nodeId: string; filename: string; sizeBytes: number; sha256: string;
  specVersion: number; trigger: BackupTriggerName; status: BackupStatus; startedAt: string; finishedAt: string | null;
  /** "directory": datos conservados antes de una restauración (no es un .tar.zst). */
  kind: "archive" | "directory";
}

/** Una copia tal y como la describe el agente. */
export interface BackupInput {
  serverId: string; filename: string; sizeBytes: number; sha256: string; specVersion: number;
  trigger: BackupTriggerName; startedAt: string; finishedAt: string | null;
}

export function toBackupDto(r: BackupRow): BackupDto {
  return {
    id: r.id, serverId: r.server_id, nodeId: r.node_id, filename: r.filename, sizeBytes: r.size_bytes, sha256: r.sha256,
    specVersion: r.spec_version, trigger: r.trigger, status: r.status, startedAt: r.started_at, finishedAt: r.finished_at,
    kind: r.trigger === "pre_restore" ? "directory" : "archive",
  };
}

export class BackupsRepo {
  constructor(private db: Db) {}

  /** Inserta o actualiza (por servidor + fichero) una copia que existe en el nodo. */
  upsertOk(nodeId: string, b: BackupInput): BackupRow {
    this.db.prepare(`INSERT INTO backups (id, server_id, node_id, filename, size_bytes, sha256, spec_version, trigger, status, started_at, finished_at, error, created_at)
      VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 'ok', ?, ?, NULL, ?)
      ON CONFLICT(server_id, filename) DO UPDATE SET node_id = excluded.node_id, size_bytes = excluded.size_bytes,
        sha256 = excluded.sha256, spec_version = excluded.spec_version, trigger = excluded.trigger, status = 'ok',
        started_at = excluded.started_at, finished_at = excluded.finished_at, error = NULL`)
      .run(newId(), b.serverId, nodeId, b.filename, b.sizeBytes, b.sha256, b.specVersion, b.trigger, b.startedAt, b.finishedAt, nowIso());
    return this.getByName(b.serverId, b.filename)!;
  }

  get(id: string): BackupRow | null {
    return (this.db.prepare("SELECT * FROM backups WHERE id = ?").get(id) as BackupRow | undefined) ?? null;
  }

  getByName(serverId: string, filename: string): BackupRow | null {
    return (this.db.prepare("SELECT * FROM backups WHERE server_id = ? AND filename = ?").get(serverId, filename) as BackupRow | undefined) ?? null;
  }

  /** Copias vigentes de un servidor, las más recientes primero. */
  list(serverId: string): BackupRow[] {
    return this.db.prepare("SELECT * FROM backups WHERE server_id = ? AND status != 'deleted' ORDER BY started_at DESC, filename DESC")
      .all(serverId) as unknown as BackupRow[];
  }

  markDeleted(serverId: string, filename: string): boolean {
    const r = this.db.prepare("UPDATE backups SET status = 'deleted' WHERE server_id = ? AND filename = ? AND status != 'deleted'").run(serverId, filename);
    return Number(r.changes) > 0;
  }

  /** Marca borradas las copias vigentes de un servidor que el nodo ya no tiene. */
  markMissingDeleted(serverId: string, present: ReadonlySet<string>): number {
    const rows = this.db.prepare("SELECT filename FROM backups WHERE server_id = ? AND status = 'ok'").all(serverId) as unknown as { filename: string }[];
    let n = 0;
    for (const r of rows) {
      if (!present.has(r.filename) && this.markDeleted(serverId, r.filename)) n++;
    }
    return n;
  }
}
```

```ts
import type { FastifyInstance } from "fastify";
import { BackupTrigger, JobStatus, type BackupCreated, type BackupDeleted, type BackupInfo, type BackupInventory, type Envelope, type JobProgress } from "@mcsm/proto";
import type { JobState } from "../jobs/repo.js";
import { nowIso } from "../lib/time.js";
import type { ServerRow } from "../servers/repo.js";
import { HttpError } from "../servers/service.js";
import { BackupsRepo, toBackupDto, type BackupDto, type BackupInput, type BackupTriggerName } from "./repo.js";

const TRIGGERS: Record<number, BackupTriggerName> = {
  [BackupTrigger.MANUAL]: "manual",
  [BackupTrigger.SCHEDULED]: "scheduled",
  [BackupTrigger.PRE_MIGRATION]: "pre_migration",
  [BackupTrigger.PRE_RESTORE]: "pre_restore",
};
const STATUSES: Record<number, JobState> = {
  [JobStatus.RUNNING]: "running",
  [JobStatus.OK]: "ok",
  [JobStatus.FAILED]: "failed",
};
const LOST = "interrumpido: el agente no tiene constancia de este job (la orden no llegó o el agente se reinició)";

const msToIso = (ms: bigint): string | null => (ms > 0n ? new Date(Number(ms)).toISOString() : null);

export function infoToInput(b: BackupInfo): BackupInput {
  return {
    serverId: b.serverId, filename: b.filename, sizeBytes: Number(b.sizeBytes), sha256: b.sha256,
    specVersion: Number(b.specVersion), trigger: TRIGGERS[b.trigger] ?? "manual",
    startedAt: msToIso(b.startedUnixMs) ?? nowIso(), finishedAt: msToIso(b.finishedUnixMs),
  };
}

/** Copias de seguridad (spec §7 BackupService): tabla `backups`, órdenes al agente e inventario. */
export class BackupService {
  /** Inicio de la última petición de inventario por nodo: solo se dan por perdidos los jobs anteriores. */
  private syncStartedAt = new Map<string, string>();

  constructor(private app: FastifyInstance, private repo: BackupsRepo) {}

  start() {
    // Tras cada Hello (no en "attached": hasta entonces el agente no atiende peticiones).
    this.app.agents.on("message", (nodeId: string, env: Envelope) => {
      if (env.body.case === "hello") void this.sync(nodeId);
    });
  }

  list(serverId: string): BackupDto[] {
    this.requireServer(serverId);
    return this.repo.list(serverId).map(toBackupDto);
  }

  /** Pide el inventario de copias y jobs al agente; llega como BackupInventory no solicitado. */
  async sync(nodeId: string): Promise<void> {
    const conn = this.app.agents.get(nodeId);
    if (!conn) return;
    this.syncStartedAt.set(nodeId, nowIso());
    try {
      await conn.request({ case: "backupList", value: { serverId: "" } }, 30_000);
    } catch (e) {
      this.app.log.warn({ nodeId, err: e }, "no se pudo pedir el inventario de copias al agente");
    }
  }

  /** Mensajes no solicitados del agente (los despacha AgentService). */
  onAgentMessage(nodeId: string, env: Envelope): void {
    switch (env.body.case) {
      case "jobProgress": return this.onJobProgress(nodeId, env.body.value);
      case "backupCreated": return this.onBackupCreated(nodeId, env.body.value);
      case "backupDeleted": return this.onBackupDeleted(nodeId, env.body.value);
      case "backupInventory": return this.onInventory(nodeId, env.body.value);
      default: return;
    }
  }

  private onJobProgress(nodeId: string, p: JobProgress) {
    const existing = this.app.jobs.getRow(p.jobId);
    if (existing) {
      if (existing.node_id !== nodeId) return; // un nodo no toca jobs de otro
    } else {
      if (!this.ownServer(nodeId, p.serverId)) return;
      // Job que inicia el propio agente (copia programada): se crea al primer aviso.
      this.app.jobs.create({ id: p.jobId, type: p.type || "backup", serverId: p.serverId, nodeId, status: "running", payload: { trigger: "scheduled" } });
    }
    this.app.jobs.update(p.jobId, { status: STATUSES[p.status] ?? "running", progress: p.progress, message: p.message, logChunk: p.logChunk });
  }

  private onBackupCreated(nodeId: string, m: BackupCreated) {
    const b = m.backup;
    if (!b || !this.ownServer(nodeId, b.serverId)) return;
    this.repo.upsertOk(nodeId, infoToInput(b));
    this.app.events.emit("backups", b.serverId);
  }

  private onBackupDeleted(nodeId: string, m: BackupDeleted) {
    if (!this.ownServer(nodeId, m.serverId)) return;
    if (this.repo.markDeleted(m.serverId, m.filename)) this.app.events.emit("backups", m.serverId);
  }

  private onInventory(nodeId: string, inv: BackupInventory) {
    const since = this.syncStartedAt.get(nodeId) ?? nowIso();
    for (const serverId of inv.serverIds) {
      if (!this.ownServer(nodeId, serverId)) continue;
      const mine = inv.backups.filter((b) => b.serverId === serverId);
      for (const b of mine) this.repo.upsertOk(nodeId, infoToInput(b));
      this.repo.markMissingDeleted(serverId, new Set(mine.map((b) => b.filename)));
      this.app.events.emit("backups", serverId);
    }
    const known = new Set<string>();
    for (const r of inv.jobs) {
      known.add(r.jobId);
      const existing = this.app.jobs.getRow(r.jobId);
      if (existing && existing.node_id !== nodeId) continue;
      if (!existing) {
        if (!this.ownServer(nodeId, r.serverId)) continue;
        this.app.jobs.create({ id: r.jobId, type: r.type || "backup", serverId: r.serverId, nodeId, status: "running", payload: { trigger: "scheduled" } });
      }
      this.app.jobs.update(r.jobId, { status: STATUSES[r.status] ?? "running", progress: r.progress, message: r.message, log: r.log });
    }
    // Jobs de copia/restauración de este nodo que el agente no conoce: se perdieron.
    for (const row of this.app.jobs.openAgentJobs(nodeId, since)) {
      if (!known.has(row.id)) this.app.jobs.finish(row.id, "failed", LOST);
    }
  }

  private ownServer(nodeId: string, serverId: string): ServerRow | null {
    const row = serverId ? this.app.servers.get(serverId) : null;
    return row && row.node_id === nodeId ? row : null;
  }

  private requireServer(id: string): ServerRow {
    const row = this.app.servers.get(id);
    if (!row) throw new HttpError(404, "Servidor no encontrado");
    return row;
  }
}
```

`panel/apps/api/src/backups/routes.ts`:
```ts
import fp from "fastify-plugin";
import type { FastifyError, FastifyInstance } from "fastify";
import { z } from "zod";
import { HttpError } from "../servers/service.js";
import { BackupsRepo } from "./repo.js";
import { BackupService } from "./service.js";

/** Decora `app.backupsService` en la raíz y registra las rutas encapsuladas de copias. */
export const backupRoutes = fp(async function backupRoutes(root: FastifyInstance) {
  const svc = new BackupService(root, new BackupsRepo(root.db));
  root.decorate("backupsService", svc);
  svc.start();
  await root.register(backupRoutesInner);
});

async function backupRoutesInner(app: FastifyInstance) {
  const svc = app.backupsService;

  app.setErrorHandler((err: FastifyError | Error, _req, reply) => {
    if (err instanceof HttpError) return reply.code(err.status).send({ error: err.message });
    if (err instanceof z.ZodError) return reply.code(400).send({ error: err.issues[0]?.message ?? "Datos inválidos" });
    const status = (err as FastifyError).statusCode;
    if (status === undefined || status >= 500) {
      app.log.error(err);
      return reply.code(500).send({ error: "Error interno" });
    }
    return reply.code(status).send({ error: err.message });
  });

  app.get<{ Params: { id: string } }>("/api/servers/:id/backups", async (req) => svc.list(req.params.id));
}

declare module "fastify" {
  interface FastifyInstance { backupsService: BackupService }
}
```

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 11; `specSchema`, `type Spec` (`@mcsm/shared`, con `backups` de la Task 2); `AgentError`, `type AgentConn`, `type Body` (`agents/hub.ts`); `toServerDto`, `toServerSpecMessage` (`servers/repo.ts`).
- Produces: `(BackupService).createNow(serverId): Promise<JobDto>`, `restore(serverId, backupId): Promise<JobDto>`, `remove(serverId, backupId): Promise<void>`, `updateSchedule(serverId, input: unknown): Promise<ServerDto>`.
- Rutas: `POST /api/servers/:id/backups` → 202 `JobDto` (tipo `backup`, `payload.trigger: "manual"`); `POST /api/servers/:id/backups/:backupId/restore` → 202 `JobDto` (tipo `restore`, `payload: { backupId, filename }`), 400 si es un pre-restore, 404 si no existe o está borrada; `DELETE /api/servers/:id/backups/:backupId` → 204; `PATCH /api/servers/:id/backups/schedule` (cuerpo = `BackupsInput`) → 200 `ServerDto` con `specVersion + 1`, 400 con el mensaje de zod. Nodo desconectado → 409 "El nodo está desconectado"; agente `busy` → 409 con su texto; otro `Error` del agente → 502 con su texto y el job `failed`. Si la orden no obtiene respuesta (desconexión o timeout), el job **sigue abierto**: el agente puede estar ejecutándolo y el inventario lo resolverá.
- `completeSpec` conserva `previous.backups` cuando el PATCH del formulario no trae `backups`.

```ts
describe("acciones sobre copias", () => {
  const post = (u: string) => app.inject({ method: "POST", url: u, headers: { cookie } });

  async function setup() {
    const { nodeId, nodeToken } = await enrollNode("nas");
    const s = app.servers.create({ nodeId, name: "Uno", slug: "uno", spec: baseSpec, desiredState: "running" });
    return { nodeId, nodeToken, s };
  }

  it("crear copia: 409 con el nodo desconectado; conectado envía BackupNow y devuelve el job", async () => {
    const { nodeToken, s } = await setup();
    let res = await post(`/api/servers/${s.id}/backups`);
    expect(res.statusCode).toBe(409);
    expect(res.json().error).toMatch(/desconectado/);
    const { agent, list } = await connectAgent(nodeToken, { [s.id]: 1n });
    agent.ack(list.id);
    const pending = post(`/api/servers/${s.id}/backups`);
    const req = await agent.waitFor("backupNow");
    if (req.body.case !== "backupNow") throw new Error();
    expect(req.body.value).toMatchObject({ serverId: s.id, trigger: BackupTrigger.MANUAL });
    const jobId = req.body.value.jobId;
    agent.ack(req.id);
    res = await pending;
    expect(res.statusCode).toBe(202);
    expect(res.json()).toMatchObject({ id: jobId, type: "backup", status: "running", serverId: s.id, payload: { trigger: "manual" } });
    const done = nextEvent(app.events, "job", (j) => j.id === jobId && j.status === "ok");
    agent.send({ body: { case: "backupCreated", value: { backup: info(s.id, "uno-20261012T040000.000Z-1.tar.zst", { trigger: BackupTrigger.MANUAL }), jobId } } });
    agent.send({ body: { case: "jobProgress", value: { jobId, serverId: s.id, type: "backup", status: JobStatus.OK, progress: 100, message: "Copia creada" } } });
    await done;
    expect((await getBackups(s.id))[0]).toMatchObject({ trigger: "manual", kind: "archive" });
    agent.close();
  });

  it("si el agente responde busy: 409 y el job queda fallido", async () => {
    const { nodeToken, s } = await setup();
    const { agent, list } = await connectAgent(nodeToken, { [s.id]: 1n });
    agent.ack(list.id);
    const pending = post(`/api/servers/${s.id}/backups`);
    const req = await agent.waitFor("backupNow");
    agent.send({ id: req.id, body: { case: "error", value: { code: "busy", message: "el servidor está ocupado con otra copia o restauración (restauración)" } } });
    const res = await pending;
    expect(res.statusCode).toBe(409);
    expect(res.json().error).toMatch(/ocupado/);
    expect(app.jobs.list({ serverId: s.id })[0]).toMatchObject({ status: "failed", message: expect.stringMatching(/ocupado/) });
    agent.close();
  });

  it("restaurar envía el nombre del fichero; los datos pre-restore no se restauran; borrar marca la copia", async () => {
    const { nodeToken, s } = await setup();
    const { agent, list } = await connectAgent(nodeToken, { [s.id]: 1n });
    const synced = nextEvent(app.events, "backups", (id) => id === s.id);
    agent.ack(list.id);
    agent.send({ body: { case: "backupInventory", value: { serverIds: [s.id], jobs: [], backups: [
      info(s.id, "uno-20261012T040000.000Z-1.tar.zst"),
      info(s.id, "data.pre-restore-20261012T060000.000Z", { trigger: BackupTrigger.PRE_RESTORE, sha256: "" }),
    ] } } });
    await synced;
    const rows = await getBackups(s.id);
    const archive = rows.find((b: any) => b.kind === "archive");
    const dir = rows.find((b: any) => b.kind === "directory");
    let res = await post(`/api/servers/${s.id}/backups/${dir.id}/restore`);
    expect(res.statusCode).toBe(400);

    const pending = post(`/api/servers/${s.id}/backups/${archive.id}/restore`);
    const req = await agent.waitFor("backupRestore");
    if (req.body.case !== "backupRestore") throw new Error();
    expect(req.body.value).toMatchObject({ serverId: s.id, filename: archive.filename });
    agent.ack(req.id);
    res = await pending;
    expect(res.statusCode).toBe(202);
    expect(res.json()).toMatchObject({ type: "restore", status: "running", payload: { backupId: archive.id, filename: archive.filename } });

    const del = app.inject({ method: "DELETE", url: `/api/servers/${s.id}/backups/${dir.id}`, headers: { cookie } });
    const dreq = await agent.waitFor("backupDelete");
    if (dreq.body.case !== "backupDelete") throw new Error();
    expect(dreq.body.value.filename).toBe(dir.filename);
    agent.ack(dreq.id);
    expect((await del).statusCode).toBe(204);
    expect((await getBackups(s.id)).map((b: any) => b.id)).toEqual([archive.id]);
    expect((await post(`/api/servers/${s.id}/backups/${dir.id}/restore`)).statusCode).toBe(404);
    agent.close();
  });

  it("la programación se valida, versiona la spec, llega al agente y el formulario de configuración no la borra", async () => {
    const { nodeToken, s } = await setup();
    const patch = (u: string, payload: object) => app.inject({ method: "PATCH", url: u, headers: { cookie }, payload });
    let res = await patch(`/api/servers/${s.id}/backups/schedule`, { enabled: true, cron: "0 4 * * MON" });
    expect(res.statusCode).toBe(400);
    expect(res.json().error).toMatch(/Día de la semana/);
    res = await app.inject({ method: "PATCH", url: `/api/servers/${s.id}/backups/schedule`, headers: { cookie } });
    expect(res.statusCode).toBe(400);

    const { agent, list } = await connectAgent(nodeToken, { [s.id]: 1n });
    agent.ack(list.id);
    const pending = patch(`/api/servers/${s.id}/backups/schedule`, { enabled: true, cron: "0 */6 * * *", keepCount: 5 });
    const apply = await agent.waitFor("applySpec");
    if (apply.body.case !== "applySpec") throw new Error();
    const sent = JSON.parse(new TextDecoder().decode(apply.body.value.server!.specJson));
    expect(sent.backups).toMatchObject({ enabled: true, cron: "0 */6 * * *", keepCount: 5 });
    agent.ack(apply.id);
    res = await pending;
    expect(res.statusCode).toBe(200);
    expect(res.json()).toMatchObject({ specVersion: 2, spec: { backups: { enabled: true, cron: "0 */6 * * *", keepCount: 5, keepDays: 7, excludes: ["logs", "cache", "*.tmp"], onlyIfPlayersSince: true } } });

    const form = patch(`/api/servers/${s.id}`, { spec: { memory: "3G" } }); // el formulario no envía backups
    agent.ack((await agent.waitFor("applySpec")).id);
    res = await form;
    expect(res.json()).toMatchObject({ specVersion: 3, spec: { memory: "3G", backups: { cron: "0 */6 * * *" } } });
    agent.close();
  });
});
```

```ts
import type { FastifyInstance } from "fastify";
import { BackupTrigger, JobStatus, type BackupCreated, type BackupDeleted, type BackupInfo, type BackupInventory, type Envelope, type JobProgress } from "@mcsm/proto";
import { specSchema, type Spec } from "@mcsm/shared";
import { AgentError, type AgentConn, type Body } from "../agents/hub.js";
import type { JobDto, JobState } from "../jobs/repo.js";
import { nowIso } from "../lib/time.js";
import { toServerDto, toServerSpecMessage, type ServerRow } from "../servers/repo.js";
import { HttpError } from "../servers/service.js";
import { BackupsRepo, toBackupDto, type BackupDto, type BackupInput, type BackupTriggerName } from "./repo.js";

const TRIGGERS: Record<number, BackupTriggerName> = {
  [BackupTrigger.MANUAL]: "manual",
  [BackupTrigger.SCHEDULED]: "scheduled",
  [BackupTrigger.PRE_MIGRATION]: "pre_migration",
  [BackupTrigger.PRE_RESTORE]: "pre_restore",
};
const STATUSES: Record<number, JobState> = {
  [JobStatus.RUNNING]: "running",
  [JobStatus.OK]: "ok",
  [JobStatus.FAILED]: "failed",
};
const LOST = "interrumpido: el agente no tiene constancia de este job (la orden no llegó o el agente se reinició)";

const msToIso = (ms: bigint): string | null => (ms > 0n ? new Date(Number(ms)).toISOString() : null);

export function infoToInput(b: BackupInfo): BackupInput {
  return {
    serverId: b.serverId, filename: b.filename, sizeBytes: Number(b.sizeBytes), sha256: b.sha256,
    specVersion: Number(b.specVersion), trigger: TRIGGERS[b.trigger] ?? "manual",
    startedAt: msToIso(b.startedUnixMs) ?? nowIso(), finishedAt: msToIso(b.finishedUnixMs),
  };
}

/** Copias de seguridad (spec §7 BackupService): tabla `backups`, órdenes al agente e inventario. */
export class BackupService {
  /** Inicio de la última petición de inventario por nodo: solo se dan por perdidos los jobs anteriores. */
  private syncStartedAt = new Map<string, string>();

  constructor(private app: FastifyInstance, private repo: BackupsRepo) {}

  start() {
    // Tras cada Hello (no en "attached": hasta entonces el agente no atiende peticiones).
    this.app.agents.on("message", (nodeId: string, env: Envelope) => {
      if (env.body.case === "hello") void this.sync(nodeId);
    });
  }

  list(serverId: string): BackupDto[] {
    this.requireServer(serverId);
    return this.repo.list(serverId).map(toBackupDto);
  }

  /** Pide el inventario de copias y jobs al agente; llega como BackupInventory no solicitado. */
  async sync(nodeId: string): Promise<void> {
    const conn = this.app.agents.get(nodeId);
    if (!conn) return;
    this.syncStartedAt.set(nodeId, nowIso());
    try {
      await conn.request({ case: "backupList", value: { serverId: "" } }, 30_000);
    } catch (e) {
      this.app.log.warn({ nodeId, err: e }, "no se pudo pedir el inventario de copias al agente");
    }
  }

  /** Mensajes no solicitados del agente (los despacha AgentService). */
  onAgentMessage(nodeId: string, env: Envelope): void {
    switch (env.body.case) {
      case "jobProgress": return this.onJobProgress(nodeId, env.body.value);
      case "backupCreated": return this.onBackupCreated(nodeId, env.body.value);
      case "backupDeleted": return this.onBackupDeleted(nodeId, env.body.value);
      case "backupInventory": return this.onInventory(nodeId, env.body.value);
      default: return;
    }
  }

  // Acciones del usuario

  /** Copia manual inmediata. Devuelve el job; el avance llega por el topic `jobs`. */
  async createNow(serverId: string): Promise<JobDto> {
    const row = this.requireServer(serverId);
    const conn = this.onlineConn(row.node_id);
    const job = this.app.jobs.create({
      type: "backup", serverId, nodeId: row.node_id, status: "running", message: "Solicitando la copia al agente", payload: { trigger: "manual" },
    });
    await this.dispatch(conn, job.id, { case: "backupNow", value: { serverId, jobId: job.id, trigger: BackupTrigger.MANUAL } });
    return this.app.jobs.get(job.id)!;
  }

  async restore(serverId: string, backupId: string): Promise<JobDto> {
    const row = this.requireServer(serverId);
    const b = this.requireBackup(serverId, backupId);
    if (b.trigger === "pre_restore") {
      throw new HttpError(400, "Son los datos que había antes de una restauración: no se restauran desde aquí. Bórralos cuando ya no los necesites.");
    }
    const conn = this.onlineConn(row.node_id);
    const job = this.app.jobs.create({
      type: "restore", serverId, nodeId: row.node_id, status: "running", message: "Solicitando la restauración al agente",
      payload: { backupId: b.id, filename: b.filename },
    });
    await this.dispatch(conn, job.id, { case: "backupRestore", value: { serverId, filename: b.filename, jobId: job.id } });
    return this.app.jobs.get(job.id)!;
  }

  async remove(serverId: string, backupId: string): Promise<void> {
    const row = this.requireServer(serverId);
    const b = this.requireBackup(serverId, backupId);
    const conn = this.onlineConn(row.node_id);
    try {
      await conn.request({ case: "backupDelete", value: { serverId, filename: b.filename } }, 60_000);
    } catch (e) {
      throw agentHttpError(e, "No se pudo borrar la copia");
    }
    this.repo.markDeleted(serverId, b.filename);
    this.app.events.emit("backups", serverId);
  }

  /** Cambia `spec.backups` (programación y retención) y la envía al agente como una spec nueva. */
  async updateSchedule(serverId: string, input: unknown) {
    const row = this.requireServer(serverId);
    const current = JSON.parse(row.spec) as Spec;
    const parsed = specSchema.safeParse({ ...current, backups: input });
    if (!parsed.success) throw new HttpError(400, parsed.error.issues[0]?.message ?? "Programación de copias inválida");
    const updated = this.app.servers.updateSpec(serverId, parsed.data);
    void this.push(updated);
    return toServerDto(updated, { includeSecrets: true });
  }

  // Internos

  private onJobProgress(nodeId: string, p: JobProgress) {
    const existing = this.app.jobs.getRow(p.jobId);
    if (existing) {
      if (existing.node_id !== nodeId) return; // un nodo no toca jobs de otro
    } else {
      if (!this.ownServer(nodeId, p.serverId)) return;
      // Job que inicia el propio agente (copia programada): se crea al primer aviso.
      this.app.jobs.create({ id: p.jobId, type: p.type || "backup", serverId: p.serverId, nodeId, status: "running", payload: { trigger: "scheduled" } });
    }
    this.app.jobs.update(p.jobId, { status: STATUSES[p.status] ?? "running", progress: p.progress, message: p.message, logChunk: p.logChunk });
  }

  private onBackupCreated(nodeId: string, m: BackupCreated) {
    const b = m.backup;
    if (!b || !this.ownServer(nodeId, b.serverId)) return;
    this.repo.upsertOk(nodeId, infoToInput(b));
    this.app.events.emit("backups", b.serverId);
  }

  private onBackupDeleted(nodeId: string, m: BackupDeleted) {
    if (!this.ownServer(nodeId, m.serverId)) return;
    if (this.repo.markDeleted(m.serverId, m.filename)) this.app.events.emit("backups", m.serverId);
  }

  private onInventory(nodeId: string, inv: BackupInventory) {
    const since = this.syncStartedAt.get(nodeId) ?? nowIso();
    for (const serverId of inv.serverIds) {
      if (!this.ownServer(nodeId, serverId)) continue;
      const mine = inv.backups.filter((b) => b.serverId === serverId);
      for (const b of mine) this.repo.upsertOk(nodeId, infoToInput(b));
      this.repo.markMissingDeleted(serverId, new Set(mine.map((b) => b.filename)));
      this.app.events.emit("backups", serverId);
    }
    const known = new Set<string>();
    for (const r of inv.jobs) {
      known.add(r.jobId);
      const existing = this.app.jobs.getRow(r.jobId);
      if (existing && existing.node_id !== nodeId) continue;
      if (!existing) {
        if (!this.ownServer(nodeId, r.serverId)) continue;
        this.app.jobs.create({ id: r.jobId, type: r.type || "backup", serverId: r.serverId, nodeId, status: "running", payload: { trigger: "scheduled" } });
      }
      this.app.jobs.update(r.jobId, { status: STATUSES[r.status] ?? "running", progress: r.progress, message: r.message, log: r.log });
    }
    // Jobs de copia/restauración de este nodo que el agente no conoce: se perdieron.
    for (const row of this.app.jobs.openAgentJobs(nodeId, since)) {
      if (!known.has(row.id)) this.app.jobs.finish(row.id, "failed", LOST);
    }
  }

  /** Envía la orden de un job. Sin respuesta (desconexión o timeout) el job sigue abierto: el agente
   * puede estar ejecutándolo y el inventario al reconectar lo aclarará. Un Error explícito lo cierra. */
  private async dispatch(conn: AgentConn, jobId: string, body: Body): Promise<void> {
    try {
      await conn.request(body, 30_000);
    } catch (e) {
      if (e instanceof AgentError && (e.code === "disconnected" || e.code === "timeout")) {
        this.app.jobs.update(jobId, { message: "El agente aún no ha confirmado la orden; el estado se actualizará cuando informe" });
        return;
      }
      const err = agentHttpError(e, "El agente rechazó la orden");
      this.app.jobs.finish(jobId, "failed", err.message);
      throw err;
    }
  }

  private async push(row: ServerRow): Promise<void> {
    const conn = this.app.agents.get(row.node_id);
    if (!conn) return; // se entregará en el próximo SyncSpecs
    try {
      await conn.request({ case: "applySpec", value: { server: toServerSpecMessage(row) } }, 120_000);
    } catch (e) {
      this.app.log.warn({ serverId: row.id, err: e }, "ApplySpec de la programación de copias sin éxito; el agente lo reintentará");
    }
  }

  private ownServer(nodeId: string, serverId: string): ServerRow | null {
    const row = serverId ? this.app.servers.get(serverId) : null;
    return row && row.node_id === nodeId ? row : null;
  }

  private requireServer(id: string): ServerRow {
    const row = this.app.servers.get(id);
    if (!row) throw new HttpError(404, "Servidor no encontrado");
    return row;
  }

  private requireBackup(serverId: string, backupId: string) {
    const b = this.repo.get(backupId);
    if (!b || b.server_id !== serverId || b.status === "deleted") throw new HttpError(404, "Copia no encontrada");
    return b;
  }

  private onlineConn(nodeId: string): AgentConn {
    const conn = this.app.agents.get(nodeId);
    if (!conn) throw new HttpError(409, "El nodo está desconectado");
    return conn;
  }
}

function agentHttpError(e: unknown, fallback: string): HttpError {
  if (!(e instanceof AgentError)) return new HttpError(502, fallback);
  if (e.code === "busy") return new HttpError(409, e.message);
  if (e.code === "disconnected") return new HttpError(409, "El nodo está desconectado");
  return new HttpError(502, e.message);
}
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { QueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { applyJobEvent, backupsKey, bindJobsLive, jobKey, jobsKey, jobTriggerLabel, retainJobsLive, upsertJob, type JobsLiveSource } from "./jobs";
import type { JobDetailDto, JobDto, ServerMessage } from "./api";

const job = (id: string, createdAt: string, over: Partial<JobDto> = {}): JobDto => ({
  id, type: "backup", serverId: "s1", nodeId: "n1", status: "running", progress: 0, message: "", payload: {},
  createdAt, updatedAt: createdAt, startedAt: createdAt, finishedAt: null, ...over,
});
const all = { serverId: null, status: null };

function fakeLive() {
  const handlers = new Map<string, (m: ServerMessage) => void>();
  const reconnect: (() => void)[] = [];
  const subscribe = vi.fn((topic: string, h: (m: ServerMessage) => void) => { handlers.set(topic, h); return () => handlers.delete(topic); });
  const live: JobsLiveSource = { subscribe, onReconnect: (cb) => { reconnect.push(cb); return () => {}; } };
  return { live, subscribe, emit: (m: ServerMessage) => handlers.get("jobs")?.(m), reconnect: () => reconnect.forEach((cb) => cb()), handlers };
}

describe("upsertJob", () => {
  const a = job("a", "2026-10-12T04:00:00.000Z");
  const b = job("b", "2026-10-12T05:00:00.000Z");
  it("inserta un job nuevo en orden (más nuevos primero)", () => {
    expect(upsertJob([a], b, all).map((j) => j.id)).toEqual(["b", "a"]);
  });
  it("sustituye el existente", () => {
    expect(upsertJob([b, a], { ...a, progress: 50 }, all).find((j) => j.id === "a")!.progress).toBe(50);
  });
  it("lo quita cuando deja de cumplir el filtro y no toca listas de otro servidor", () => {
    expect(upsertJob([b, a], { ...a, status: "ok" }, { serverId: null, status: "running" }).map((j) => j.id)).toEqual(["b"]);
    const other = [job("x", "2026-10-12T03:00:00.000Z", { serverId: "s2" })];
    expect(upsertJob(other, a, { serverId: "s2", status: null })).toBe(other);
  });
});

describe("applyJobEvent", () => {
  it("actualiza las listas en caché y añade el trozo de log al detalle", () => {
    const qc = new QueryClient();
    const a = job("a", "2026-10-12T04:00:00.000Z");
    qc.setQueryData(jobsKey({ serverId: "s1" }), [a]);
    qc.setQueryData(jobsKey({ status: "running" }), [a]);
    qc.setQueryData<JobDetailDto>(jobKey("a"), { ...a, log: "save-off\n" });
    applyJobEvent(qc, { type: "job", job: { ...a, progress: 40 }, logChunk: "save-all flush\n" });
    expect(qc.getQueryData<JobDto[]>(jobsKey({ serverId: "s1" }))![0]!.progress).toBe(40);
    expect(qc.getQueryData<JobDetailDto>(jobKey("a"))!.log).toBe("save-off\nsave-all flush\n");
  });
  it("al terminar un job recarga las copias de su servidor y su detalle", () => {
    const qc = new QueryClient();
    const spy = vi.spyOn(qc, "invalidateQueries");
    applyJobEvent(qc, { type: "job", job: job("a", "2026-10-12T04:00:00.000Z", { status: "ok" }), logChunk: "" });
    expect(spy).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ queryKey: backupsKey("s1") });
    expect(spy).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ queryKey: jobKey("a") });
  });
});

describe("bindJobsLive / retainJobsLive", () => {
  it("un aviso de copias recarga la lista de ese servidor; al reconectar recarga todo", () => {
    const qc = new QueryClient();
    const spy = vi.spyOn(qc, "invalidateQueries");
    const f = fakeLive();
    const off = bindJobsLive(qc, f.live);
    f.emit({ type: "backups", serverId: "s1" });
    expect(spy).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ queryKey: backupsKey("s1") });
    f.reconnect();
    expect(spy).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ queryKey: ["jobs"] });
    expect(spy).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ queryKey: ["backups"] });
    off();
    expect(f.handlers.has("jobs")).toBe(false);
  });
  it("varias vistas comparten una sola suscripción", () => {
    const qc = new QueryClient();
    const f = fakeLive();
    const r1 = retainJobsLive(qc, f.live);
    const r2 = retainJobsLive(qc, f.live);
    expect(f.subscribe).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    r1();
    r1(); // liberar dos veces no cuenta doble
    expect(f.handlers.has("jobs")).toBe(true);
    r2();
    expect(f.handlers.has("jobs")).toBe(false);
  });
});

    expect(jobTriggerLabel({ payload: {} })).toBeNull();
    expect(jobTriggerLabel({ payload: { trigger: "raro" } })).toBeNull();
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Caché en vivo y hooks**

`panel/apps/web/src/lib/jobs.ts`:
```ts
import type { QueryClient, QueryKey } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import type { BackupTriggerName, JobDetailDto, JobDto, JobState, ServerMessage } from "./api";

export interface JobsFilter { serverId?: string; status?: JobState }
type NormalizedFilter = { serverId: string | null; status: JobState | null };
type JobMsg = Extract<ServerMessage, { type: "job" }>;
type BackupsMsg = Extract<ServerMessage, { type: "backups" }>;

export const JOB_STATUS_LABEL: Record<JobState, string> = {
  queued: "En cola", running: "En curso", ok: "Correcto", failed: "Fallido", cancelled: "Cancelado",
};
export const TRIGGER_LABEL: Record<BackupTriggerName, string> = {
  manual: "Manual", scheduled: "Programada", pre_migration: "Antes de migrar", pre_restore: "Antes de restaurar",
};
const JOB_TYPE_LABEL: Record<string, string> = { backup: "Copia de seguridad", restore: "Restauración" };
const MAX_LIST = 200;

export const jobTypeLabel = (type: string): string => JOB_TYPE_LABEL[type] ?? type;
export const isActive = (j: Pick<JobDto, "status">): boolean => j.status === "queued" || j.status === "running";

/** Etiqueta del origen de una copia guardado en el payload del job (`trigger`), o null. */
export function jobTriggerLabel(j: Pick<JobDto, "payload">): string | null {
  const t = j.payload?.trigger;
  return typeof t === "string" && t in TRIGGER_LABEL ? TRIGGER_LABEL[t as BackupTriggerName] : null;
}

export const jobsKey = (f: JobsFilter = {}): QueryKey => ["jobs", { serverId: f.serverId ?? null, status: f.status ?? null }];
export const jobKey = (id: string): QueryKey => ["job", id];
export const backupsKey = (serverId: string): QueryKey => ["backups", serverId];

const matches = (j: JobDto, f: NormalizedFilter) => (!f.serverId || j.serverId === f.serverId) && (!f.status || j.status === f.status);

/** Inserta o actualiza un job en una lista (más nuevos primero) respetando su filtro. */
export function upsertJob(list: JobDto[], job: JobDto, f: NormalizedFilter): JobDto[] {
  const rest = list.filter((j) => j.id !== job.id);
  if (!matches(job, f)) return rest.length === list.length ? list : rest;
  return [...rest, job].sort((a, b) => (a.createdAt < b.createdAt ? 1 : a.createdAt > b.createdAt ? -1 : 0)).slice(0, MAX_LIST);
}

export function applyJobEvent(qc: QueryClient, msg: JobMsg): void {
  for (const [key, data] of qc.getQueriesData<JobDto[]>({ queryKey: ["jobs"] })) {
    if (!data) continue;
    const f = (key[1] ?? {}) as Partial<NormalizedFilter>;
    const next = upsertJob(data, msg.job, { serverId: f.serverId ?? null, status: f.status ?? null });
    if (next !== data) qc.setQueryData(key, next);
  }
  qc.setQueryData<JobDetailDto>(jobKey(msg.job.id), (old) => (old ? { ...old, ...msg.job, log: old.log + msg.logChunk } : old));
  if (!isActive(msg.job)) {
    void qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: jobKey(msg.job.id) }); // log final completo
    if (msg.job.serverId) void qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: backupsKey(msg.job.serverId) });
  }
}

export function applyBackupsEvent(qc: QueryClient, msg: BackupsMsg): void {
  void qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: backupsKey(msg.serverId) });
}

/** Subconjunto de `Realtime` que se usa aquí (facilita los tests). */
export interface JobsLiveSource {
  subscribe(topic: string, handler: (msg: ServerMessage) => void): () => void;
  onReconnect(cb: () => void): () => void;
}

/** Conecta la caché con el topic `jobs`. Devuelve la función de limpieza. */
export function bindJobsLive(qc: QueryClient, live: JobsLiveSource): () => void {
  const offs = [
    live.subscribe("jobs", (m) => {
      if (m.type === "job") applyJobEvent(qc, m);
      else if (m.type === "backups") applyBackupsEvent(qc, m);
    }),
    // Durante la desconexión se pierden avisos: recargamos.
    live.onReconnect(() => {
      void qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["jobs"] });
      void qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["job"] });
      void qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["backups"] });
    }),
  ];
  return () => offs.forEach((off) => off());
}

const bindings = new WeakMap<QueryClient, { count: number; off: () => void }>();

/** Como bindJobsLive pero con recuento: varias vistas montadas comparten una sola suscripción
 * (si no, cada trozo de log se añadiría varias veces al detalle). */
export function retainJobsLive(qc: QueryClient, live: JobsLiveSource): () => void {
  let b = bindings.get(qc);
  if (!b) {
    b = { count: 0, off: bindJobsLive(qc, live) };
    bindings.set(qc, b);
  }
  b.count++;
  const binding = b;
  let released = false;
  return () => {
    if (released) return;
    released = true;
    binding.count--;
    if (binding.count === 0) {
      binding.off();
      bindings.delete(qc);
    }
  };
}
```

`panel/apps/web/src/hooks/use-jobs.ts`:
```ts
import { useEffect } from "react";
import { useQuery, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { api, type JobDetailDto, type JobDto } from "@/lib/api";
import { realtime } from "@/lib/ws";
import { jobKey, jobsKey, retainJobsLive, type JobsFilter } from "@/lib/jobs";

export function useJobs(filter: JobsFilter = {}) {
  const params = new URLSearchParams({ limit: "100" });
  if (filter.serverId) params.set("serverId", filter.serverId);
  if (filter.status) params.set("status", filter.status);
  return useQuery({ queryKey: jobsKey(filter), queryFn: () => api.get<JobDto[]>(`/api/jobs?${params}`) });
}

export function useJob(id: string) {
  return useQuery({ queryKey: jobKey(id), queryFn: () => api.get<JobDetailDto>(`/api/jobs/${id}`) });
}

/** Mantiene jobs y copias al día con el topic `jobs` mientras el componente esté montado. */
export function useJobsLive() {
  const qc = useQueryClient();
  useEffect(() => retainJobsLive(qc, realtime), [qc]);
}
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { formToSchedule, presetFor, scheduleToForm } from "./backups";

describe("programación de copias", () => {
  it("sin programación muestra los valores por defecto desactivados", () => {
    expect(scheduleToForm(undefined)).toEqual({
      enabled: false, cron: "0 4 * * *", keepDays: "7", keepCount: "10", excludes: "logs\ncache\n*.tmp", onlyIfPlayersSince: true,
    });
  });
  it("reconoce los presets", () => {
    expect(presetFor("0 4 * * *")).toBe("daily");
    expect(presetFor(" 0 */6 * * * ")).toBe("6h");
    expect(presetFor("15 3 * * 1")).toBe("custom");
  });
  it("convierte el formulario y limpia las exclusiones", () => {

    expect(r).toEqual({ ok: true, value: { enabled: true, cron: "* * * * *", keepDays: 0, keepCount: 3, excludes: ["logs", "*.jar"], onlyIfPlayersSince: false } });

    expect(formToSchedule({ ...scheduleToForm(undefined), keepDays: "siete" })).toMatchObject({ ok: false, error: expect.stringMatching(/enteros/) });
    expect(formToSchedule({ ...scheduleToForm(undefined), excludes: "../world" })).toMatchObject({ ok: false });
  });
```

`panel/apps/web/src/lib/backups.ts`:
```ts
import { backupsSchema, type BackupsConfig } from "@mcsm/shared";

export const CRON_PRESETS = [
  { value: "daily", label: "Cada día a las 4:00", cron: "0 4 * * *" },
  { value: "6h", label: "Cada 6 horas", cron: "0 */6 * * *" },
  { value: "hourly", label: "Cada hora", cron: "0 * * * *" },
  { value: "weekly", label: "Cada domingo a las 4:00", cron: "0 4 * * 0" },
  { value: "custom", label: "Personalizada", cron: "" },
] as const;

/** Preset que corresponde a una expresión ("custom" si ninguno). */
export function presetFor(cron: string): string {
  return CRON_PRESETS.find((p) => p.cron !== "" && p.cron === cron.trim())?.value ?? "custom";
}

/** Estado del formulario de programación (los números como texto, las exclusiones una por línea). */
export interface ScheduleForm {
  enabled: boolean; cron: string; keepDays: string; keepCount: string; excludes: string; onlyIfPlayersSince: boolean;
}

export function scheduleToForm(b: BackupsConfig | undefined): ScheduleForm {
  const d = b ?? backupsSchema.parse({});
  return {
    enabled: d.enabled, cron: d.cron, keepDays: String(d.keepDays), keepCount: String(d.keepCount),
    excludes: d.excludes.join("\n"), onlyIfPlayersSince: d.onlyIfPlayersSince,
  };
}

/** Valida con el mismo esquema que el panel y el agente. */
export function formToSchedule(f: ScheduleForm): { ok: true; value: BackupsConfig } | { ok: false; error: string } {
  const num = (s: string) => (s.trim() === "" ? 0 : Number(s.trim()));
  const keepDays = num(f.keepDays);
  const keepCount = num(f.keepCount);
  if (!Number.isInteger(keepDays) || !Number.isInteger(keepCount)) {
    return { ok: false, error: "Los días y el número de copias deben ser números enteros" };
  }
  const r = backupsSchema.safeParse({
    enabled: f.enabled, cron: f.cron.trim(), keepDays, keepCount, onlyIfPlayersSince: f.onlyIfPlayersSince,
    excludes: f.excludes.split("\n").map((l) => l.trim()).filter((l) => l !== ""),
  });
  if (!r.success) return { ok: false, error: r.error.issues[0]?.message ?? "Programación inválida" };
  return { ok: true, value: r.data };
}
```

```tsx
import type { JobDto } from "@/lib/api";
import { jobTypeLabel } from "@/lib/jobs";

export function ProgressBar({ value }: { value: number }) {
  const v = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, value));
  return (
    <div className="h-1.5 w-full overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-zinc-800" role="progressbar" aria-valuemin={0} aria-valuemax={100} aria-valuenow={v}>
      <div className="h-full bg-emerald-500 transition-[width]" style={{ width: `${v}%` }} />
    </div>
  );
}

/** Tarjeta con el job en curso (copia o restauración). */
export function JobProgress({ job }: { job: JobDto }) {
  return (
    <div className="rounded-md border border-zinc-800 p-3 text-sm" aria-live="polite">
      <div className="mb-2 flex justify-between gap-2">
        <span>{jobTypeLabel(job.type)} en curso: {job.message || "…"}</span>
        <span className="tabular-nums text-zinc-400">{job.progress}%</span>
      </div>
      <ProgressBar value={job.progress} />
    </div>
  );
}
```
